|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 3B B=62 | 1.086 (1.041 to 1.143) | 1.082 (1.057 to 1.113) | 1.094 (1.069 to 1.124) | 1.085 (1.075 to 1.094) | 1.080 (1.071 to 1.087) | amort_split_500 | 0.922 | 1.088, 1.087 |
| 3B B=125 | 1.071 (1.052 to 1.092) | 1.074 (1.063 to 1.085) | 1.076 (1.066 to 1.088) | 1.063 (1.059 to 1.068) | 1.057 (1.052 to 1.061) | amort_split_1000 | 0.940 | 1.069, 1.066 |
| 7B-AWQ B=62 | 1.087 (1.053 to 1.120) | 1.064 (1.046 to 1.080) | 1.060 (1.047 to 1.073) | 1.060 (1.048 to 1.073) | 1.047 (1.040 to 1.054) | amort_split_250 | 0.943 | 1.046, 1.053 |
| 7B-AWQ B=125 | 1.059 (1.046 to 1.075) | 1.047 (1.035 to 1.060) | 1.034 (1.021 to 1.047) | 1.025 (1.013 to 1.038) | 1.016 (1.006 to 1.027) | amort_split_500 | 0.975 | 1.025, 1.025 |

Steady state and blocks (carry_plain / best fixed, fold A, arm chosen for H = 60; last 12 of 60 with the arm chosen on the last 12: 1.048 (1.040 to 1.056), 1.042 (1.035 to 1.048), 1.020 (1.010 to 1.033), 0.982 (0.974 to 0.990), all four cells in table order; last 12 of 120: 1.065, 1.058, 1.027, 0.979). Block ratios, batches 1-12 / 13-24 / 25-36 / 37-48 / 49-60: 3B B=62 1.259, 1.034, 1.052, 1.057, 1.061; 3B B=125 1.187, 1.029, 1.037, 1.042, 1.042; 7B B=62 1.100, 1.032, 1.053, 1.069, 1.052; 7B B=125 1.090, 1.007, 1.009, 1.015, 1.009. The edge is front-loaded: the first 12 batches carry 1.09 to 1.26 and the later blocks 1.01 to 1.07. Post-calibration best / carry_plain at H = 60: 0.953, 0.966, 0.956, 0.989 (the last CI reaches 1.001). Extrapolation in 1/H (four prefix points, descriptive only): asymptotes of best / carry 0.911 to 0.935 (3B), 0.953 to 0.962 (7B B=62), 0.979 to 0.980 (7B B=125); the fit puts the fall of carry / best through 1.03 at H of about 52 to 58 in 7B B=125 only (measured 1.034 at H = 36 and 1.025 at 60) and the 0.99 line nowhere; the measured pooled best / carry at H = 120 in that cell is 0.987 against 0.976 fitted, so the 1/H fit underestimates the shrinkage there.

Verdict. Regression gate: PASS (19,968 per-batch values over 1,664 vectors, every one of the 25 arms plus batch_base_errors in all 64 runs/L7b files, 0 differences; 0 in-job self-check failures in all 256 job files; re-derived with separate inline code in this session). Pre-registered rule at H = 60, fold A: PASS cells 3 of 4 (3B B=62, 3B B=125, 7B B=62; 7B B=125 is 1.025 and misses 1.03), KILL cells 0 of 4 (best / carry 0.922, 0.940, 0.943, 0.975); swapped fold 3 and 0; pooled 16 3 and 0. Formal outcome: VOID, because the memorisation control fired and cannot be satisfied at H = 60. outcome_if_control_waived (result.json) is PASS.

Memorisation control (fixed in advance: mean over the 16 stream seeds of AUC of the final carry_plain fit on prompts it labelled minus AUC on prompts it never labelled, after batch 60; > 0.02 in any cell triggers a rerun with already-paid prompts removed from later candidate pools). Primary gap per cell: 0.026 (6 of 16 seeds above 0.02), 0.013 (6 of 16), 0.313 (16 of 16), 0.332 (16 of 16): triggered in 3B B=62, 7B B=62 and 7B B=125. The statistic is confounded by selection: the labelled prompts are the learner's own high-score picks and the never-labelled set is the low-score region, where the AUC is near or below chance (never-labelled AUC 0.464 and 0.471 in the 7B cells, labelled 0.777 and 0.803), so the large 7B gaps measure range restriction, not memory. A split-half control added by this run separates the two (distinct labelled prompts split at random, rule refit on one half's labels, AUC on that half minus AUC on the other half): in-sample minus held-out 0.057, 0.019, 0.041, 0.023 on the iid streams (15, 9, 14, 10 of 16 seeds above 0.02), and 0.005, 0.004, 0.008, 0.004 on the no-repeat streams, so repeated prompts cost the learner's fit a small, real in-sample optimism (0.02 to 0.06 AUC) that a 10-feature logistic should not have on fresh prompts. The pre-registered statistic is reported as specified; the split-half one is a supplement and does not override the trigger.

The remedy cannot reach H = 60. Carry pays B labels per batch, so 60 batches need 3,720 (B = 62) or 7,500 (B = 125) paid prompts from a table of 3,000, and on the iid streams 72 to 78 percent of carry_plain's picks (74 to 79 percent of its hits) re-pay a prompt it already labelled; its 5,409 to 5,879 hits at B = 125 are four times the 1,313 to 1,419 error prompts the whole table contains. The remedy was run anyway (mode iid_nr; one common stream for all arms defined by carry_plain's payments, since the spec does not say whose payments: batch i draws 250 with replacement from the prompts carry has not paid for, the stream ends when fewer than 250 remain). It reached 49 to 51 batches at B = 62 and 25 at B = 125, and the stream is far from stationary (mean errors per batch in the last 12 batches 9.0, 39.7, 17.3, 42.7 against 108.0, 98.9, 99.6, 92.2 in the first 12). Carry / best on that stream against the iid value at the same H (pooled 16, no-repeat minus iid): 3B B=62 +0.008 (H = 12), +0.071 (24), +0.089 (36), +0.071 (49); 3B B=125 +0.006 (12), -0.022 (24), -0.023 (25); 7B B=62 +0.017 (12), -0.030 (24), -0.075 (36), -0.110 (49; fold A 0.909, the best fixed arm ahead of the learner); 7B B=125 -0.032 (12), -0.058 (24), -0.056 (25; fold A 0.995). So the iid ratio is not invariant to removing repeats: the 7B edge shrinks by 0.03 to 0.11 and reaches or crosses parity, the 3B B=62 edge grows, 3B B=125 moves by 0.02. Because the remedy stream is depleted, this is a sensitivity probe and not a replacement for the H = 60 test.

Self-check (separate inline code from the raw hz_*.json, bootstrap seed 777): carry_plain / best fixed at H = 12 and 60 for the 4 cells reproduced the summarizer to 4 decimals (CI ends within 0.001); the gate was recomputed (0 differences in 19,968 values); the hz120 files reproduce the 60-batch run exactly on every arm for all 64 jobs; the no-repeat streams' repeat share is 10.2 to 10.5 percent, i.e. within-batch duplicate draws only, none across batches.

What it changes about the thesis claims. The finite-horizon route is mostly closed: the best fixed arm reaches 0.99 x carry_plain in 0 of 4 cells at H = 60, and in the two 3B cells and 7B B=62 the ratio is flat from H = 24 to H = 120 (1.05 to 1.09), so the persistent learner's 6 to 9 percent edge is not a 12-batch artifact there. 7B B=125 is the exception: the cumulative edge decays 1.059, 1.047, 1.034, 1.025, 1.016 over H = 12, 24, 36, 60, 120 and the steady-state ratio is 0.982 and 0.979 (learn-then-freeze at n = 500 to 1000 matches or beats the learner after calibration), so for the 7B judge at B = 125 claim 2 is close to a finite-horizon effect. Because the control fired and the remedy is infeasible on a 3,000-prompt table, none of this is licensed as a clean long-horizon result: the table has only 1.3 to 1.4 thousand error prompts, so horizons beyond about 10 to 25 batches are dominated by re-catching known errors, and the one sensitivity probe available moves the 7B cells toward parity. Phase 1 reading, provisional: persistent carry for the 3B judge and for 7B at B = 62, learn-then-freeze at n of 500 to 1000 (amort_split / amort_strat) is within 2 percent of the learner in steady state at 7B B = 125.

Caveats. (1) Duplicated prompts with identical weak-judge labels, and 72 to 78 percent of the learner's picks re-pay a known prompt; fixed arms re-pay too, but the learner fits on a larger duplicated label set. (2) No drift by construction (most favourable to a once-fitted rule). (3) One population, one task (GSM8K judge_error from E04), offline replay on weak-judge labels, the 3B judge barely passed the E04 validity gate. (4) Stream-seed CIs cover stream and tie-break variation only, every stream draws from the same 3,000 prompts, so they are optimistic; each fold has 8 seeds. (5) The 1/H extrapolation is a two-parameter line through four prefix points and is descriptive only. (6) n = 1000 at B = 62 needs k = 17 calibration batches; it wins the last-12 selection in 4 of 4 cells (amort_split_1000 in three, amort_strat_1000 in 7B B=125), so the selection set is at its upper edge. (7) The no-repeat remedy uses one common stream defined by the learner's payments, an interpretation of the spec, and carries within-batch duplicates (about 10 percent).

Follow-ups (ranked; full specs in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md under Proposed by the loop, iteration 10).
1. L10a, fresh-prompt long-horizon stream (GPU, needs Rustom's override of the E04 stop rule, the same blocker as L7c): extend the prompt table with at least 10,000 more GSM8K prompts, rollouts and judge features, so that 60 batches at B = 125 need no re-paid prompt; the only way to retire the memorisation question and the finite-table error supply. High value, blocked.
2. L10b, corrected control and per-arm cache regime (CPU, about 10 min): replace the pre-registered statistic by the split-half in-sample minus held-out gap with a threshold set from the no-repeat baseline (about 0.005), and rerun the iid streams with a per-arm label cache (a known prompt is free, not counted, and the budget refills with the next-ranked prompt) to a horizon of about 25 batches. Medium value; decides whether the 7B parity in the no-repeat probe is real.
3. L8a, sign-flip drift, unchanged and still queued: with the horizon question narrowed to 7B B = 125, drift is the remaining route by which a persistent learner beats a frozen rule.

## L9a 2026-10-18 inconclusive
(Loop-level entry written after re-deriving the headline, the gate, the control statistics and the no-repeat probe from the raw hz_*, gate_*, nr_* JSONs in runs/L9a with separate inline code. The run-level entry above, line 788, carries the long audit text, block tables and the no-repeat probe. The formal pre-registered outcome is VOID; it is mapped to "inconclusive" here because every job and the gate ran cleanly, so nothing failed technically, but the pre-registered validity control cannot be satisfied at H = 60, so neither the pass rule nor the kill rule is licensed. The rule applied with the control waived reads PASS in 3 of 4 cells and KILL in 0 of 4 and is reported below as a conditional reading, not as the verdict.)

Hypothesis. Claim 2's 12-batch edge (carry_plain beats the best fixed arm by 1.06 to 1.14 in L6a and L7b) is a finite-horizon effect: 12 batches is the whole 3,000-prompt table, and the L7b equal-label result (frozen designed rule 0.987 to 1.018 of a frozen learner rule at equal n) leaves horizon as the only route by which a one-shot calibrated rule can reach the learner. Falsifier (kills claim 2 under stationarity): on iid-with-replacement streams of 60 batches of 250, the best fixed arm (amort_strat / split / seed at n in 125, 250, 500, 1000, chosen on stream seeds 0 to 7) reaches >= 0.99 x carry_plain in at least 3 of 4 cells (2 judges x B in 62, 125). Pass: carry_plain / best fixed >= 1.03 with the bootstrap CI lower bound above 1 in at least 3 of 4 cells. VOID if the regression gate fails or the memorisation control (AUC of the final carry_plain fit on prompts it labelled minus on prompts it never labelled, > 0.02 in any cell) cannot be satisfied.

What was run. CPU only, no generation, no GPU memory (E04 stop rule untouched); launched with bin/gpu_run.sh avrl/runs/L9a 'bash exp/L9a.sh', 13.5 min wall, exit_code 4 (the summarizer's code for "control void"; run.log is empty and no job log contains a Traceback). Task GSM8K, 3,000-prompt table, label judge_error from the E04 feature tables of Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-AWQ, ten cheap + judge features. Stream: each of H = 60 batches draws m = 250 prompts uniformly with replacement (rng 30000 + stream seed, policy seed = stream seed), per-step budget B = 62 and 125, stream seeds 0 to 15, 64 verdict jobs, plus three optional 64-job stages: gate (disjoint12 = the L7b layout), nr (the no-repeat remedy), hz120 (the same streams to H = 120). Arms: 12 fixed arms amort_{strat,split,seed}_{125,250,500,1000} (calibration charged and counted as hits, k = ceil(n / B) calibration batches, one balanced logistic fit reused free), references carry_plain, thr_agreement, uniform, diagnostic freeze_plain_n. Selection as L7b: best fixed arm per cell and per window chosen on stream seeds 0 to 7, reported on 8 to 15 (verdict fold A), swapped fold and pooled 16 as cross-checks, percentile bootstrap over stream seeds.

Numbers (cumulative errors caught over 60 batches, mean of 16 stream seeds; ratios at H = 60 on verdict fold A):

Verification by the loop (recomputed from the raw JSONs in this session; not taken from the run report). (1) Regression gate: 64 gate files against runs/L7b, all 25 arms plus batch_base_errors, 19,968 per-batch values, 0 differences. (2) Verdict fold: best arms, carry_plain / best at H = 12 and 60, bootstrap CIs (seed 0, 4,000 resamples, ends within 0.002 of result.json) and the swapped-fold ratios (1.088, 1.069, 1.046, 1.025) reproduce for all four cells. (3) Pre-registered memorisation statistic and the split-half supplement reproduce from the per-job memorisation blocks (0.026 / 0.057, 0.013 / 0.019, 0.313 / 0.041, 0.332 / 0.023). (4) No-repeat streams end at 49 to 51 batches (B = 62) and 25 (B = 125) and are depleted: mean errors per batch in the first 12 versus last 12 batches are 108.0 vs 9.0, 98.9 vs 39.7, 99.6 vs 17.3, 92.2 vs 42.7. (5) Last-12 arm choice and ratios (1.048, 1.042, 1.020, 0.982) reproduce. (6) The H = 60 verdict-fold arm is below carry_plain on 8 of 8, 8 of 8, 8 of 8 and 7 of 8 report seeds. (7) A per-seed oracle (best of the 12 fixed arms on each seed, selection-optimistic by construction) over all 16 seeds averages 0.940, 0.949, 0.953, 0.996 of carry_plain and reaches 0.99 on 0, 0, 0 and 14 of 16 seeds (max 0.967, 0.968, 0.981, 1.011): even with hindsight selection only the 7B B=125 cell is at parity.

Verdict against the pre-registered thresholds: inconclusive (formally VOID). Gate passed with 0 differences. The pre-registered control fired in 3 of 4 cells (3B B=62, 7B B=62, 7B B=125) and its prescribed remedy cannot reach H = 60: carry pays B labels per batch, so 60 batches need 3,720 or 7,500 paid prompts from a 3,000-prompt table that holds only 1,313 (7B) to 1,419 (3B) error prompts, and 72 to 78 percent of carry_plain's picks re-pay a prompt it already labelled. The conditional reading with the control waived: the pass rule holds in 3 of 4 cells (7B B=125 is 1.025 and misses 1.03) on fold A, swapped fold and pooled 16, and the kill rule holds in 0 of 4 (best / carry 0.922 to 0.975). The kill is not close in three cells; the one that comes close, 7B B=125, decays 1.059, 1.047, 1.034, 1.025, 1.016 over H = 12, 24, 36, 60, 120 with a last-12 ratio of 0.982 (0.979 at H = 120). I do not call this a pass because the pre-registered statistic is a range-restriction-confounded proxy (the 7B never-labelled AUC is 0.46 to 0.47 because the unlabelled set is the low-score region, so the 0.31 and 0.33 gaps are mostly not memory), but the split-half gap is a real if small in-sample optimism (0.02 to 0.06 AUC against 0.004 to 0.008 without repeats), and the only probe that removes repeats moves both 7B cells by -0.03 to -0.11 toward parity (fold A 0.909 at B = 62, H = 49; 0.995 at B = 125, H = 25) on a depleted stream. The sign of that shift is evidence about the 7B cells that the headline table cannot reflect, so a pass would overstate it and a kill is not supported either.

What it changes about the thesis claims. Claim 2 (a learned allocator admits fewer corrupted rewards than any fixed rule at equal budget) is not a 12-batch artifact in the 3B cells and in 7B B=62 on this population: the best fixed arm stays at 0.91 to 0.96 of carry_plain from H = 12 to H = 120 and carry_plain stays 2 to 6 percent ahead in the steady state (last 12 batches, 1.020 to 1.061 depending on whether the fixed arm is chosen on the last 12 or on H = 60), with the first 12 batches (1.09 to 1.26) front-loading the edge. It is close to parity in 7B B=125 (steady state 0.98, hindsight-oracle fixed arm 0.996), so there claim 2 is mostly a short-horizon and re-paid-prompt effect. Two readings the loop adds from the table. (a) freeze_plain_1000 reaches 0.983 to 0.991 of carry_plain in three cells and 1.003 in the fourth, so refitting past the first ~1,000 of the learner's own labels is worth at most 1 to 2 percent here; what the learner has that every designed or seeded fixed arm lacks is an error-rich label stream earned while calibrating (L7b's finding), which holds at long horizons but is not "continued learning". (b) The claim stays supported only for the pre-registered fixed family, a stationary stream, and one weak-judge population; drift (L8a) and a stream without re-paid prompts (L10b, L10a) are now the two remaining ways to kill or confirm it. Phase 1 recipe, provisional: learn online for the first ~1,000 labels, then freezing costs 0 to 2 percent (3B, 7B B=62: persistent carry is slightly better; 7B B=125: freeze is equal), with thr_agreement still the committed fallback (carry_plain is 1.13 to 1.17 times thr_agreement at H = 60). Claim 3 (the edge comes from structure in prompt space) is untouched: L9a uses ten rollout and judge statistics and no prompt-space features, and E01 and E04 remain KILL for it on GSM8K.

Caveats. (1) Duplicated prompts with identical weak-judge labels; carry_plain's picks re-pay a known prompt 72 to 78 percent of the time and the fixed arms re-pay too, but the learner fits on a larger duplicated label set, so the direction of the bias is not known a priori and only the no-repeat probe speaks to it. (2) No drift by construction (most favourable to a once-fitted rule). (3) One population and task, offline replay on weak-judge labels; the 3B judge barely passed the E04 validity gate. (4) Stream-seed CIs cover stream and tie-break variation only (every stream draws from the same 3,000 prompts), so they are optimistic; each fold has 8 seeds; the batch-level and swapped-fold checks agree. (5) The 1/H extrapolation is a four-point two-parameter line and is descriptive only; at 7B B=125 it underestimates the measured H = 120 shrinkage (fitted best / carry 0.976, measured 0.987). (6) The no-repeat probe uses one common stream defined by carry_plain's payments (an interpretation of the spec) and is depleted, so it is a sensitivity probe, not a replacement for the H = 60 test. (7) n = 1000 wins the last-12 selection in 4 of 4 cells, so the selection set is at its upper edge. (8) carry_plain gained an optional label-list argument (None in every pre-existing mode); the gate confirms the pre-existing behaviour is unchanged.

Follow-ups (ranked by expected information value; L10a and L10b are already specced in lit/avrl/EXPERIMENT_BACKLOG.md under Proposed by the loop, iteration 10; L10c is new).
1. L10b, per-arm label cache plus the corrected split-half control (CPU, about 10 min, no override): the highest-value CPU experiment because it is the only one whose outcome is not already implied by existing numbers. The L9a no-repeat probe at H = 24 gives carry / best 1.162, 1.058, 1.005, 0.995 (fold A), so its kill rule (best fixed >= 0.99 x carry_plain at H = 24 in at least 2 of 4 cells) will likely fire in both 7B cells while the 3B cells decide pass versus kill-by-count; it separates "claim 2 holds everywhere" from "claim 2 holds for the 3B judge only".
2. L10c, table-size dose-response on the existing table (CPU, about 10 min, no override): rerun the L9a iid streams on fixed random subtables of N = 750, 1500, 3000 prompts so the repeat share rises as N falls. If carry / best at H = 60 rises by >= 0.03 from N = 3000 to N = 750 in at least 3 of 4 cells, the iid edge is partly re-catching known errors and the 7B parity is the no-repeat limit; if it is flat within 0.02, re-pay is not the driver and L10a is not worth the E04 override. Medium to high value because it keeps the payment model fixed (L10b changes it) and needs no new generation.
3. L8a, sign-flip drift (CPU, about 10 min), unchanged and still queued: with the horizon question narrowed to 7B B=125, drift is the remaining route by which a persistent learner beats a frozen rule; the freeze_plain_1000 / carry_plain ratio of 0.983 to 1.003 here is the stationary baseline it must beat. L10a (fresh-prompt long-horizon stream, GPU) stays blocked on Rustom's E04 override and should wait for L10b and L10c.

**Critic note (L9a, adversarial review 2026-10-18; covers both L9a entries above).** The formal VOID stands (mapped to "inconclusive" in the loop-level entry), and so does every verdict count: gate 0 differences, control triggered in 3B B=62, 7B B=62 and 7B B=125, and with the control waived fold A PASS cells 3 of 4, KILL cells 0 of 4 (swapped fold 3 and 0, pooled 16 3 and 0). The corrections below are one provenance time, one unsupported causal reading of the split-half supplement (which also invalidates a threshold proposed for L10b), one over-strong use of the no-repeat probe, and two scope or wording fixes; none moves a verdict count. What I re-derived with separate code (exp/l9a_critic.py, exp/l9a_critic_split.py; bootstrap seed 777, 10,000 resamples): all 256 JSONs exist and their budget, order_seed, mode, m = 250, ten-column feature list and label judge_error match the file names; every arm has 60 (hz), 12 (gate), 120 (hz120) or 49 to 51 and 25 (nr, B = 62 and 125) values; every per-batch value lies between 0 and min(B, batch_base_errors); 0 failed in-job checks; batch_base_errors are identical across B for the same judge and stream seed; the hz120 prefix equals hz on every arm in all 64 jobs; job logs have no Traceback, warning or nan, run.log is empty, exit_code is 4. Gate: 1,664 vectors, 19,968 values, all 25 arms plus batch_base_errors, 0 differences against runs/L7b, no extra arms; an AST comparison with exp/l7b_design.py shows all 11 copied functions identical except carry_plain (the optional L argument), with memo new. Determinism: I re-ran hz 3B B=62 s5, nr 7B B=125 s9 and gate 7B B=62 o3 into runs/L9a_critic/; arms, base errors, arm_info, memorisation and stream blocks equal the stored files, which supports the entry's "first pass overwritten, identical files" statement. Verdict fold: best arms, carry / best at H = 12, 24, 36, 60, 120 (fold A, swapped, pooled 16), CIs (within 0.001 of result.json), best / thr_agreement, post-calibration ratios, last-12 and block ratios, the all-16 totals (3263.6 / 5879.3 / 3197.9 / 5409.3 for carry_plain, and thr_agreement and uniform), freeze_plain ratios (0.984, 0.983, 0.991, 1.003), the 8, 8, 8, 7 of 8 below-carry counts, the per-seed oracle (0.940, 0.949, 0.953, 0.996; 0, 0, 0, 14 of 16 at 0.99; max 0.967, 0.968, 0.981, 1.011), the memorisation block (gaps 0.026, 0.013, 0.313, 0.332 with 6, 6, 16, 16 seeds above 0.02; AUCs; repeat shares 71.5 to 77.9 percent), no-repeat stream lengths, first and last 12 errors per batch, every no-repeat minus iid difference, the 1/H asymptotes and the 52 and 58 crossings, and the measured 0.987 at H = 120: all match. No leakage: the allocator columns are the ten cheap and judge columns (largest single-feature AUC against judge_error 0.814, 0 NaN), the judge sees the question and the extracted number only, gold enters only through judge_error. Budget accounting is equal (every arm picks B positions per batch, calibration labels are charged and counted as hits, score-based arms all pay each duplicate copy of a prompt). Sample size: the three conditional-pass cells stay above 1.03 if the 8-seed CI half-widths (at most 0.013) are doubled (lowest 1.060 minus 0.025 = 1.035), and 7B B=125 (1.025, CI 1.013 to 1.038) misses 1.03 on the point estimate in all three folds.

Corrections. (1) Provenance: done.flag and summary.log were written at 23:24:04, so the wall time is 23:10:43 to 23:24:04, 13.4 minutes, not "23:24:16" and 13.5. (2) The split-half supplement does not show that repeated prompts cause the in-sample optimism, so "repeated prompts cost the learner's fit a small, real in-sample optimism (0.02 to 0.06 AUC against 0.004 to 0.008 without repeats)" is unsupported. I re-ran carry_plain on iid streams 0 to 3 for 3B B=62 and 7B B=125 (20 random halves each) and refit the split-half rule once per distinct prompt, so the fit contains no repeats: the gap is 0.057, 0.057, 0.068, 0.067 against 0.052, 0.054, 0.059, 0.052 with the learner's multiplicities in 3B B=62, and 0.007, 0.005, 0.016, 0.021 against 0.013, 0.014, 0.017, 0.025 in 7B B=125. So the iid gap is ordinary finite-sample overfit of a 10-feature logistic on a small, class-imbalanced set of distinct labelled prompts (872 to 931 prompts, 84 percent errors, 140 to 156 non-errors in 3B B=62) and the contrast with the no-repeat streams (about 2,780 distinct prompts, 51 percent errors) confounds set size and class balance with repeats. The sentence and the contrast should be dropped as memorisation evidence in both entries and in the backlog's iteration 10 paragraph, and the L10b threshold "set from the no-repeat baseline (about 0.005)" would be invalid: its baseline must be the unit-weight split-half gap at matched distinct-set size and error share. Memorisation is neither shown nor excluded (72 to 78 percent of picks re-pay a known prompt); the VOID stands because the pre-registered statistic exceeds 0.02 in 3 of 4 cells and its remedy cannot reach H = 60, not because memorisation was demonstrated. (3) The no-repeat probe is dominated by depletion and should not be read as evidence about the 7B cells. In its last 12 batches the stream holds 9.0, 39.7, 17.3 and 42.7 errors per batch (cells in table order, B = 62, 125, 62, 125), fewer than B, and the share of those errors caught is carry_plain 36, 90, 53, 86 percent, thr_agreement 76, 93, 52, 86 percent, amort_strat_250 50, 92, 71, 94 percent: the label-free rule catches as many as or more than the learner in all four cells, because the remaining errors are by construction the ones carry_plain's own paid set scored low. The shift of -0.03 to -0.11 toward parity in the 7B cells is therefore what this stream produces mechanically and says nothing about whether the iid 7B edge re-catches known errors; "the sign of that shift is evidence about the 7B cells" should read "uninformative about memorisation". The prediction in follow-up 1 that the L10b kill rule "will likely fire in both 7B cells" rests on these numbers and is untested; the per-arm cache regime of L10b (no depletion) is the actual test. (4) Scope and wording. The loop-level "What it changes" paragraph says claim 2 "is not a 12-batch artifact in the 3B cells and in 7B B=62" without the run-level sentence "none of this is licensed as a clean long-horizon result"; under the formal VOID it is a conditional reading (control waived), on one 3,000-prompt table with re-paid prompts allowed. "Steady state 0.98, hindsight-oracle fixed arm 0.996" in the same paragraph pairs a carry / best number (fixed arm ahead by 1.8 percent, last 12 batches, arm chosen on them) with a best / carry number from a per-seed oracle (a different arm on each seed, selection-optimistic); the best single fixed arm chosen in hindsight on all 16 seeds is amort_split_500 at best / carry 0.979 over 60 batches (0.924, 0.941, 0.950 in the other cells).

Corrected verdict: VOID (formal; "inconclusive" in the loop-level mapping), unchanged. Conditional reading with the control waived: PASS in 3 of 4 cells (3B B=62, 3B B=125, 7B B=62; carry / best 1.085, 1.063, 1.060) and KILL in 0 of 4, with 7B B=125 (1.025, falling 1.059 to 1.016 over H = 12 to 120, last-12 ratio 0.98) the one cell near parity. Neither a pass nor a kill is licensed: the pre-registered memorisation control cannot be satisfied at H = 60 on a 3,000-prompt table, memorisation itself is not demonstrated by the supplement, and the no-repeat probe is uninformative because the stream is depleted.

## L1a 2026-10-19 kill (claim 3 on GSM8K in the agreement-free rollout-level regime; the widened pre-registered kill rule holds on both rungs, the literal backlog kill rule does not fire and is reported separately)
Hypothesis: claim 3 died on GSM8K only for group-level proxies, because group agreement (AUC 0.78 to 0.91 for proxy error) stayed a free cheap feature. If every one of the 24,000 rollouts is its own decision and the allocator is denied every group aggregate, the cheap signal drops to judge_p plus per-rollout features (OOF AUC about 0.70), and prompt-space structure (embedding PCs, cells) may then carry usable information. Falsifier (kills the revised thesis 'the allocator wins only where the proxy lacks a confidence signal' on GSM8K): adding the 16 PCs or the OOF cell encoding to the agreement-free cheap model adds under 0.02 and 0.01 AUC on both the 3B and 7B-AWQ judge rungs, and the cell100 layout (ts_online) does not beat the same-feature layout-free learner (greedy_online) by more than 3 percent at 25 percent budget.

Run: CPU only, no GPU memory used (E04 stop rule untouched). Launched with bin/gpu_run.sh runs/L1a 'bash exp/L1a.sh' at 23:55 EDT on 2026-10-18, exit_code 0, 248 s wall (build 2 s, regression gate 9 s, structure 13 s, 72 replay jobs about 225 s with xargs -P 24, summary 1 s); no Traceback in any log. Files: runs/L1a/result.json (all metric values), summary.md (every table), verify.json, structure_<table>.json and structure.json, build.json, tab_*.parquet (six tables), sim_<table>_<cheap|pc>_<cell100|cell30|pid>_s<split>.json (72), gate/. Scripts (all new, copies of L1b where noted): exp/l1a_build.py, l1a_sim_alloc.py (copy of l1b_sim_alloc.py plus a rollout mode), l1a_gate_check.py (copy of l1b_gate_check.py), l1a_structure.py, l1a_summarize.py, l1a_verify.py, L1a.sh. The p0 scripts are untouched. Before the real launch I ran build, one structure table, a 20 percent pid-subsample dry run of all 72 replay jobs plus the summarizer, and the group-mode gate in the shell, then deleted those outputs; the 2,000-row timing job took 8.9 s (cell100) and 4.3 s (pid).

Design. Each of the 24,000 rollouts is a row. Joined on (pid, answer) with the E04 judge scores (0 unmatched, 0 duplicate pairs) and on pid with log_qlen, cell30/100/300 and pc0..pc15. cheap_r (8 features): judge_logit (logit of judge_p clipped to 1e-6), log(tokens+1), truncated, has_decimal, is_negative, log10(|answer|+1), empty_answer, log_qlen. No statistic over the 8 rollouts of a prompt appears in any table or feature list (asserted in the build and in every sim job; l1a_verify.py re-checks all 72 sim files and 6 tables). Agreement is read only inside l1a_structure.py, in memory, for the labelled positive control. Accept rule q: judge_p >= the (1 - 0.6421) quantile of judge_p over all 24,000 rollouts (0.6421 = overall rollout accuracy; the only gold-derived scalar, it fixes the accept rate); secondary rule p05: judge_p > 0.5 on 7B-AWQ. Labels: corrupted = accepted and wrong by gold, population = accepted rollouts only; judge_wrong = (accepted != correct) over all 24,000 rollouts. Replay: K = 1, 8 policy seeds, budgets 10 / 25 / 50 percent of the sim population, split seeds 0 to 3 for cell100 (cells split whole, 25 percent of cells to the fit set, 75 percent to the sim set, so the 8 rollouts of a prompt never straddle the boundary), split 0 for cell30 and for the pid layout.

Gates. Regression gate: group mode of l1a_sim_alloc.py on runs/E04/feats_{E01,Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct,Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-AWQ}.parquet (cell100, cheap features, split 0) reproduces runs/E04/sim_*_cell100_cheap.json with 108 policy-budget cells compared and 0 per-seed differences. Build gate: positives per table 4,484 / 8,693 (3B q corrupted / judge_wrong), 3,958 / 7,888 (7B q), 2,053 / 9,632 (7B p05), all >= 200; rollout-level judge_p AUC for wrong 0.65400 (3B) and 0.70196 (7B-AWQ), which round to 0.654 and 0.702. Accept counts 15,686 (3B) and 15,439 (7B) match the pre-run probe; at judge_p > 0.5 the 3B judge accepts only 920 rollouts (90 corrupted), which is why q is the primary rule.

Structure (OOF AUC, 5 folds assigned by pid, 3 CV seeds, balanced logistic on standardized features; delta against cheap_r; [95 percent pid bootstrap, 200 resamples]; null = pid permutation of the PC and cell assignment, 20 permutations, CV seed 0, mean +- SD). Primary label corrupted:

| quantity | 3B (primary) | 7B-AWQ (confirmation) | 7B-AWQ p05 (secondary rule) |
|---|---|---|---|
| positives / accepted rollouts (rate) | 4,484 / 15,686 (28.6%) | 3,958 / 15,439 (25.6%) | 2,053 / 9,885 (20.8%) |
| judge_logit alone (CV logistic) | 0.586 | 0.620 | 0.600 |
| cheap_r | 0.7045 | 0.7090 | 0.6910 |
| delta, + 16 PCs | +0.0097 [+0.0043, +0.0149], null -0.0029 +- 0.0012 | +0.0084 [+0.0021, +0.0152], null -0.0036 +- 0.0012 | +0.0054 [-0.0028, +0.0132], null -0.0082 +- 0.0022 |
| delta, cell30 encoding | +0.0001 [-0.0005, +0.0008] | +0.0011 [-0.0004, +0.0027] | +0.0006 [-0.0007, +0.0017] |
| delta, cell100 encoding | +0.0007 [-0.0005, +0.0018] | +0.0013 [-0.0004, +0.0029] | +0.0019 [-0.0005, +0.0043] |
| delta, cell300 encoding | -0.0004 [-0.0010, +0.0001] | +0.0006 [-0.0007, +0.0021] | -0.0002 [-0.0034, +0.0027] |
| delta, HistGradientBoosting + PCs (1 seed) | +0.0116 [-0.0000, +0.0226] | +0.0118 [-0.0004, +0.0219] | +0.0064 [-0.0109, +0.0197] |
| cheap_r + agreement (positive control, ceiling only) | 0.8662 (+0.1618) | 0.8677 (+0.1586) | 0.8586 (+0.1676) |
| share of the agreement gain recovered by the PCs | 6.0% | 5.3% | 3.2% |

Shrinkage-80 cell encodings (about 10 prompts, robustness) change nothing: at most +0.0023. Secondary label judge_wrong (all 24,000 rollouts): cheap_r 0.6495 / 0.6579 / 0.6829 (3B / 7B q / 7B p05), PC delta -0.0021 / -0.0033 / -0.0059, cell deltas between -0.0011 and +0.0001, agreement control adds only +0.026 / +0.022 / +0.009. The PC delta on the corrupted label is real in the sense that the pid bootstrap CI excludes zero on 3B and 7B q and the permutation null sits at -0.003 to -0.004 (so the net signal is about +0.012), but it is 5 to 6 percent of what agreement provides and under the 0.02 line.

Allocation replay, primary label (corrupted), cell100, 25 percent budget, mean errors caught over 4 split seeds x 8 policy seeds (sim population 10,900 to 12,000 rollouts holding 2,800 to 3,500 corrupted; columns cheap_r | cheap_r + PCs where two numbers are given):

| arm | 3B | 7B-AWQ |
|---|---|---|
| uniform | 838 | 733 |
| best label-free threshold (thr_judge_low at 25 and 50 percent, thr_tokens_long at 10 percent) | 1,047 | 1,002 |
| best recal_charged (n_cal 400 in every split) | 1,401 \| 1,340 | 1,275 \| 1,219 |
| static_matched | 1,283 \| 1,277 | 1,141 \| 1,132 |
| greedy_online (no layout) | 1,415 \| 1,432 | 1,290 \| 1,284 |
| ts_online (cell100 layout) | 1,433 \| 1,429 | 1,285 \| 1,277 |
| static (free labels, upper bound) | 1,479 \| 1,466 | 1,334 \| 1,311 |
| oracle | 2,934 | 2,835 |

Ratios at 25 percent (pooled over 32 runs; z = pooled gap over sqrt(a^2 + b^2) of the run SDs; per-split ratios in brackets), cheap_r | cheap_r + PCs:
- ts_online / greedy_online: 3B 1.013 (z 0.44; splits 1.021, 0.993, 1.032, 1.004) | 0.998; 7B 0.996 (z -0.10) | 0.994. At 10 percent 0.997 | 0.980 (3B), 1.004 | 0.983 (7B); at 50 percent 1.000 | 0.992 and 1.003 | 0.990. The largest single-split layout edge is +3.2 percent (3B split 2, cheap_r only, z 3.4), 1.8 points under the 1.05 bar, and it is not present with the PCs or on 7B.
- greedy_online(cheap_r + PCs) / greedy_online(cheap_r): 3B 1.012 (z 0.38; splits 1.010, 1.010, 1.008, 1.021), 7B 0.996.
- learned / best label-free threshold: ts 3B 1.369 | 1.365, 7B 1.282 | 1.274; greedy 3B 1.352 | 1.369, 7B 1.287 | 1.281; z 5.9 to 9.5. 10 percent 1.31 to 1.39, 50 percent 1.10 to 1.20.
- learned / best recal_charged: greedy 3B 1.010 | 1.069, 7B 1.011 | 1.053; ts 1.022 | 1.066 and 1.008 | 1.047; z 0.2 to 1.7. The cheap_r + PCs ratios are higher only because the 24-feature fixed rule fit on 100 to 400 labels is worse (3B recal 1,340 vs 1,401), not because the PC learner is better (1,432 vs 1,415).
- greedy / static (free labels): 0.957 | 0.977 (3B), 0.967 | 0.980 (7B).
Other layouts and labels. cell30 (split 0, 25 percent): ts/greedy 0.990 | 1.005 (3B), 0.975 | 0.992 (7B). Secondary tables at cell100, 25 percent, ts/greedy cheap_r | +PCs: 3B judge_wrong 1.006 | 0.981, 7B judge_wrong 1.001 | 0.959, 7B p05 corrupted 1.024 | 1.027, 7B p05 judge_wrong 1.047 | 0.998; none reaches 1.05 and the largest pooled z is 0.8. The pid (sibling) layout, a mechanism check and not a verdict arm: ts/greedy at 25 percent is 1.108 | 1.088 (3B corrupted), 1.091 | 1.100 (7B), 1.120 | 1.158 (7B p05) and 1.18 to 1.26 on the judge_wrong labels, all with z of 3.6 or more, i.e. an edge exists only when the bucket is the prompt itself.

Verification by the loop (exp/l1a_verify.py, plain-numpy recomputation from the raw per_seed lists, the p0 and E04 parquets, and sklearn GroupKFold folds that differ from the script's; 34 checks, 0 mismatches). Pooled ts/greedy, greedy/threshold, ts/threshold, greedy/recal, greedy(pc)/greedy(cheap) and the split counts at 25 percent reproduce to 1e-9 on both rungs; positives and the two judge AUCs reproduce; an independent GroupKFold gives cheap_r 0.7048 (3B) and 0.7093 (7B) and a PC delta of +0.0088 and +0.0073 against the script's +0.0097 and +0.0084 (fold assignment only); the KILL and not-PASS flags recompute from the stored deltas and ratios.

Verdict against the pre-registered thresholds: the gate passes (0 differences, all positives >= 200, AUCs reproduce), so the run is not void. PASS does not hold: (a) fails on both rungs (PC delta 0.0097 and 0.0084 against 0.05, best cell delta 0.0013 against 0.02) and (b) fails (0 of 4 split seeds have ts_online >= 1.05 x greedy_online with a gap above 2 SD, on either feature set, on either rung). KILL holds on both rungs: (a') PC delta < 0.02 with CI upper end 0.0149 / 0.0152 < 0.03 and all three cell deltas < 0.01 (largest +0.0013); (b') pooled ts_online / greedy_online 1.013 | 0.998 (3B) and 0.996 | 0.994 (7B), all <= 1.03, and greedy_online(cheap_r + PCs) / greedy_online(cheap_r) 1.012 and 0.996, both <= 1.03. Outcome: KILL for the revised thesis on GSM8K at cheap AUC about 0.70. The literal backlog rule does not fire: its kill needs delta AUC < 0.01 (3B +0.0097, 7B +0.0084, met, the 3B one by 0.0003) and cell delta < 0.01 (met) and learned <= 1.05 x the best label-free threshold at 25 percent (not met: 1.27 to 1.37, z 5.9 to 9.5); its pass needs delta AUC >= 0.05 or cell delta >= 0.02 (not met). Under the literal text the result is therefore neither a pass nor a kill; the reason is item (i).

Items the spec requires me to state. (i) Deviation from the backlog text: the backlog pass compared 'ts_online or greedy_online' with a label-free threshold at 1.10x, but greedy_online combining cheap_r (AUC 0.70) against single-feature thresholds on judge_p (AUC 0.59 to 0.62) is a feature-combination effect (claim 2 material), not prompt-space structure, so the claim-3 verdict uses the layout and PC increments instead. Learned over best label-free threshold (1.27 to 1.37 at 25 percent) and over best recal_charged (1.01 to 1.07) are reported as a claim-2 point in the agreement-free regime (a new G03 point at cheap AUC 0.7045 and 0.7090). (ii) The delta AUC kill line was widened from 0.01 to 0.02 because the pre-run probe found +0.008 and +0.010; the measured +0.0097 and +0.0084 confirm a 0.01 line would have been a coin flip (the 3B point misses it by 0.0003); the literal 0.01 rule is also reported above. (iii) The pid-layout result is a mechanism check, not a verdict arm: the edge there (ts/greedy 1.09 to 1.16 on the corrupted label) is sibling-label autocorrelation, which in real GRPO is available for free through group agreement or pass rate, so it cannot rescue claim 3 for the thesis. (iv) Agreement was removed by ablation, not by changing the task, so a null here is weaker than a null on real code; per the E04 critic it should not reopen the E04 kill, and it does not.

Choices I made where the spec left room (all fixed before the data were seen, none tuned afterwards). (b) of PASS is evaluated on both feature sets and passing on either counts, whereas (b') of KILL requires both to be <= 1.03, so the asymmetry is against KILL. 'Best label-free threshold' and 'best recal_charged' are the per (split, budget) max over their families on the same 8 seeds, which can only favour the fixed rule. Target-encoding shrinkage 10 is in rollout rows as specified (a shrinkage-80 column is added as robustness). The structure stage ran before the replay jobs, as in the spec; the regression gate was also run once in the shell before launch.

Caveats. (1) One policy model (Qwen2.5-1.5B), one task, one answer-only judge that never sees the reasoning; the accept rate is fixed from the overall rollout accuracy (a disclosed gold-derived scalar). (2) Rollouts of a prompt are strongly dependent (shared answers, hence shared labels); CV is grouped by pid and the replay splits whole cells, but the effective sample size is closer to the 3,000 prompts than the 24,000 rows, which is why the bootstrap resamples pids. (3) Policy-seed SD does not cover split variance; the 4 split ratios are reported; at 25 percent they range from 0.986 to 1.032 and their SD is at most 0.017. (4) The 8-feature logistic and the HistGradientBoosting arm may miss nonlinear prompt-space structure that only a text-reading scorer would capture; the HGB delta (+0.012, CI touching 0) is the same size as the linear one, so there is no sign of hidden nonlinear structure here. (5) Absolute budgets are about 5x larger than in L1b (about 2,900 labels at 25 percent against about 560), so 400 calibration labels are a smaller share of the budget and the claim-2 ratios here are not on the same footing as L1b's; the result that a charged 8-feature rule reaches 99 percent of greedy_online at 25 percent should not be compared to L1b's 1.037 to 1.072 without that caveat. (6) A pre-registered threshold family of four single-feature rules is a weak label-free baseline compared with the multi-feature charged rule, which is the right baseline for claim 2 and the reason the claim-3 verdict does not use it.

What it changes. Claim 3 (edge from structure in prompt space) is now unsupported on GSM8K for group-level proxies (E04) and for rollout-level proxies with group aggregates ablated (L1a): in the regime without free agreement, 16 PCs add +0.008 to +0.010 AUC (about 5 to 6 percent of the agreement gain, CI above zero on the primary label, under the 0.02 line), cell encodings add at most +0.0013, and the replay shows no usable layout or PC gain (ts/greedy 0.994 to 1.013, greedy with PCs / without 0.996 to 1.012). The revised thesis 'the allocator wins only where the proxy lacks a confidence signal' is killed on GSM8K at cheap AUC about 0.70; the only structure that helps a bucketed learner is the prompt itself (pid layout), which is the group-agreement channel the thesis assumed away. Claim 2 gets one new point: in this regime a persistent learner beats single-feature label-free rules by 1.27 to 1.37 at 25 percent but beats a charged 8-feature recalibrated rule by only 1.01 (3B, 7B) and reaches 0.96 to 0.97 of the free-label static upper bound; the L1b edge of 1.04 to 1.07 over the charged rule does not reappear at rollout level with larger budgets. By the spec's reading, L1a does not justify asking Rustom to override the E04 stop rule for L1c (GPU); the one thing L1a cannot speak to is a trained reward model that scores full reasoning, whose errors need not be a function of the answer or of cheap rollout features, so L1c's prior is lowered rather than zeroed.

Follow-ups. (1) No further GSM8K claim-3 variants on this machine; the deliverable stays the proxy-error measurement, the fixed-threshold recipe and the persistent-learner recipe. (2) Optional claim-2 check on the same data (CPU, about 5 min): rerun the rollout replay on a random 2,250-rollout subsample of the sim population so that absolute budgets match L1b (about 560 labels at 25 percent) and see whether the learner's edge over the charged rule returns (budget-scale explanation) or stays near 1.01. (3) Claim 3 in a regime with a genuinely agreement-free proxy and real prompt heterogeneity needs a task that is not on disk (code, open-ended); that is a data problem, not an allocator problem.

## L1a 2026-10-19 kill
(Loop-level entry written after recomputing the headline numbers from the raw sim_*.json, structure_*.json, tab_*.parquet and gate/gate_check.json in runs/L1a with separate code, not from the run report. The run-level entry above, line 872, carries the long audit text. Scope of the kill: claim 3 on GSM8K, one 1.5B policy, answer-only judge proxies, agreement removed by ablation.)

Hypothesis. Claim 3 died on GSM8K only for group-level proxies, because group agreement (AUC 0.78 to 0.91 for proxy error) stayed a free cheap feature. If every one of the 24,000 rollouts is its own decision and the allocator is denied every group aggregate, the cheap signal drops to judge_p plus per-rollout features (OOF AUC about 0.70) and prompt-space structure (embedding PCs, cells) may then carry usable information. Falsifier of the revised thesis ("the allocator wins only where the proxy lacks a confidence signal") on GSM8K: 16 PCs or the OOF cell encoding add under 0.02 and 0.01 AUC to the agreement-free cheap model on both judge rungs, and the cell100 layout (ts_online) does not beat the same-feature layout-free learner (greedy_online) by more than 3 percent at 25 percent budget.

What was run. CPU only, no GPU memory, launched through bin/gpu_run.sh runs/L1a 'bash exp/L1a.sh', exit_code 0, 248 s wall (build 2 s, gate 9 s, structure 13 s, 72 replay jobs 247 s total with xargs -P 24, summary 1 s). Data: 3,000 GSM8K prompts x 8 rollouts of Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct (24,000 rollouts) joined to the Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct (primary) and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-AWQ (confirmation) answer-only judge scores from runs/E04. Accept rule: judge_p at or above the quantile that accepts 64.21 percent (the overall rollout accuracy, a disclosed gold-derived scalar); primary label corrupted = accepted and wrong by gold (15,686 rows, 4,484 positives on 3B; 15,439 and 3,958 on 7B); secondary labels judge_wrong and the 7B judge_p > 0.5 rule. Structure: 5 folds assigned by pid, 3 CV seeds, balanced logistic on 8 per-rollout features (cheap_r), 200 pid-bootstrap resamples, 20 pid-permutation nulls, one HGB seed. Replay: K = 1, 8 policy seeds, budgets 10 / 25 / 50 percent, cell100 on split seeds 0 to 3, cell30 and the pid (sibling) layout on split 0. Regression gate: 108 policy-budget cells against runs/E04, 0 per-seed differences. The six tab_*.parquet files contain no forbidden aggregate column (checked from the column lists).

Numbers (primary label corrupted; AUC out of fold; deltas against cheap_r, [95 percent pid bootstrap]):

|---|---|---|---|
